# Pattern index (MSVC 5.0 /O2 — Gruntz)

One line per pattern: `- [title](file.md) — cN — tags — symptoms` (cN = confidence /10).
Sorted by primary tag so families cluster. Grep this file by tag (`cpp:switch`,
`cpp:eh`, `topic:wall`) or by symptom token, then read only the hits. Schema +
how-to-search: [`README.md`](README.md). Authoring: new pattern = new file + one
line here, SAME commit.

WALL vs STEERABLE: `topic:codegen-idiom`/`topic:flags` = a source spelling closes the
diff; `topic:wall`/`topic:scoring-artifact` = code already correct / no source form —
stop chasing (orchestration §2a).

- [A small header inline was transcribed once per call site - find them with `inline_clones`](shared-inline-transcribed-once-per-call-site.md) - c10 - cpp:inline cpp:global cpp:call | asm:imul asm:lea asm:cmp | topic:identity topic:codegen-idiom - the same 8-20 instruction block verbatim in many unrelated functions, or an odd immediate (`0x269ec3`, `0x939`) recurring at unrelated RVAs, or N near-identical `static inline` helpers under N different local names in N `.cpp`s. Whoever reconstructs a TU in isolation sees the EXPANSION, not the source, so a header inline gets open-coded once per site. `python -m gruntz.audit.inline_clones --consts` histograms non-address immediates (the `.reloc` table says which are pointers; a magic multiply's addend hides in a `lea` displacement); `--ngrams -n 8` clusters register-alpha-renamed instruction shingles. Decisive confirmation is the STRING COUNT: under /Gf a literal written at 60 sites occupies 60 addresses, one emitted from a shared header COMDAT-folds to ONE - all nineteen act keys have exactly one address each. Calibration points (already-shared helpers it re-finds): zDArray::ResolveEntry, LOGIC_WORKER_PUMP, CMapMgr::CellFlagsAt. GetRandomNumber 14 copies/11 TUs, act-name registrar 61 copies/34 TUs
- [`if (X) { ... return K; } else { return K; }` IS `||` - the TOTAL exit-merge regime](if-else-both-arms-return-is-the-or-regime.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:if cpp:return cpp:goto | asm:xor asm:jcc asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - every `return 0` collapses onto ONE sunk epilogue even though the source uses separate `if (c) return 0;` guards, and no `||` or `&&` greps in the source. A nested-return `if` with an else-arm returning the SAME value is a FOURTH spelling of the TOTAL regime and is byte-for-byte identical to `||` (measured: cells A and C both 2 rets / 63 insns, flat sequential guards 6 rets). Its cost lands on UNRELATED guards far away, so the symptom appears nowhere near the cause. Flatten it, then choose the regime you want - retail's guarded serialise/init functions are PARTIAL, i.e. `goto fail;`. CTriggerMgr::ScanGroup 0x7a760 89.48 -> 99.19 (31/31 blocks, 20/20 branch targets). Also a correctness-bug detector: the same `else` was failing every overlay-less save, where retail skips the Serialize call and falls into the success tail. Screen: `python -m gruntz.audit.exit_merge_sieve --over`
- [`goto fail;` is the ONLY spelling that shares SOME exits and duplicates the rest](goto-fail-shares-one-exit-block.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:goto cpp:return | asm:xor asm:jcc asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - base has MORE ret blocks than target (DUP-EXIT): retail's leading guards `je` one shared exit while ours each fall through to their own copy, and the shared block keeps the `xor eax,eax` ours elides. cl 5.0 /O2 has exactly THREE exit-merging regimes chosen by the SOURCE construct - separate `if (c) return 0;` merges NOTHING, `goto fail;` + a trailing `fail: return 0;` merges the goto sites and leaves every other `return 0` its own copy, and `||`/`&&`/a positive-gate nest collapses EVERY same-valued return in the whole function into one sunk block. Nothing else moves them: not /GX, body size, jump distance or statement order. The elided `xor` is a CONSEQUENCE (a one-predecessor exit reached with the value in eax costs 2 bytes, which is what tips cl into duplicating) - give the block a second predecessor and the xor returns; named zero local, (BOOL)0, FALSE, NULL, volatile, `== 0` and an intervening store were all measured and none change it. cl inverts the LAST goto's branch, so the guard you send to `fail` last decides where the shared block lands. Screen: `python -m gruntz.audit.exit_merge_sieve --dup`. CSBI_ImageSetAni::Init 0xe7980 88.49 -> 96.43, CSBI_StatzTabGruntBar::BuildMultiplayerTabStatusBar 0xea1f0 88.56 -> 94.68, CSBI_GruntMachine::BuildResourceTabStatusBar 0xe8a70 85.91 -> 93.17, CDDrawSubMgrPages::TransEnter 0x158e40 88.21 -> 97.31. PICK THE SPELLING BY WHERE RETAIL'S SHARED BLOCK SITS: mid-body => `goto`, sunk past the success return => `||` (CPlay::OnLButtonDblClk 0xce660 86.53 -> 90.35 with goto, -> 95.70 with `||`); the label's SOURCE position is irrelevant. The shared block need not be a bare return - five copies of `{ m_defenderState = AISTATE_SEEK; return 1; }` behind one `seek:` label took CGrunt::StepArrivalDefense 0xf2b20 76.85 -> 83.09 and StepArrivalDefenseLean 0xf8240 54.22 -> 63.47. A surplus ret is ALSO a correctness-bug detector: CTriggerMgr::DestroyGroup 0x798d0 86.63 -> 100 EXACT (a `return 0` outside its guard block) and CGrunt::OnStruck 0x588f0 75.48 -> 90.39 (a spurious `else`). NOT this lever when the duplicated block is a /GX unwind epilogue (`mov fs:0,ecx`) - that is a scheduling-driven cross-jump both compilers coin-flip, and six spellings were measured on CLatencyList::FillCombo 0x37ff0 with none better. The MIRROR direction (retail has more exits, 45 functions) is NOT solved and /Os is not a lever (pragma optimize("s") scored 88.49 -> 45.07)

- [A `goto` whose label is just the CODE THAT FOLLOWS is a transcription bug, not a lever](goto-continuation-label-is-not-a-shared-exit.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:goto cpp:return | asm:jcc asm:jmp asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - DUP-EXIT where our shared block sits mid-function and retail's sits after the whole if/else, and the block skeleton aligns for the first N blocks then permutes. Only a label reached ONLY by goto is the `goto fail` lever; a label that is simply the NEXT STATEMENT after the if/else is redundant, and cl HOISTS it to the last goto's position, mis-ordering the whole second half. Delete the gotos, make each guard a positive gate, let the arm fall through. Two companions from the same function: read WHICH SIDE retail falls through to (`jcc Bshort | fall Blong` vs ours `jcc Blong | fall Bshort` means the arms are swapped in the source), and `&&` short-circuits a member load retail issues unconditionally (hoist `i32 row = m_tileRow; i32 col = m_tileCol;` above the bounds `if`). CStaticHazard::LoadAttributes 0xfc1a0 52.65 -> 98.91 (the goto step alone 70.40 -> 98.10); the arm swap also moved CGrunt::StepGooSuckerBehavior 0xf0e20 46.38 -> 47.85 and CGrunt::StepBrickLayerBehavior 0xecc90 57.18 -> 58.92
- [A `push` of a callee-saved register BELOW the entry guard means the source had exactly ONE tail return](shrink-wrapped-prologue-needs-one-tail-return.md) - c10 - cpp:branch cpp:local | asm:push asm:pop | topic:codegen-idiom - base is +2/+4 instructions on a guarded function and every register rotates: retail pushes only what the ENTRY GUARD uses, sinks the rest to their first definition (in first-use order, not the canonical ebx/ebp/esi/edi), and the early-out pops fewer. cl 5.0 shrink-wraps only when the guard's `return` is the SOLE early return - one more `return` anywhere in the body pins every save into the prologue. The decision is made on the source flow graph and survives tail duplication, so a retail function with six `ret`s is still shrink-wrapped. Fix: nested if/else with ONE tail return. Sieve: leading-push count base vs target, 33 candidates tree-wide. DSoundCloneInst::GetItem 0x135d70 90.31 -> 100.00 EXACT, CGrunt::UpdateGruntStatus 0x617c0 94.63 -> 100.00 EXACT, CDDrawWorkerHost::DeactivateDistantObjects 0x163370 87.88 -> 100.00 EXACT, CPlay::StepGridWalk 0xd0a60 66.67 -> 100.00 EXACT (breaks tail-block-placement-cross-jump-wall)
- [A `return` inside the null guard is what lets cl home a local in the dead parameter slot](null-guard-early-return-frees-the-parameter-home.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:local cpp:call | asm:cmp asm:xor asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - a tiny guarded-call accessor plateaus in the 60s with the SAME instruction count as retail, every register rotated: retail reads the parameter into a register as instruction 0 (before the first `push`), guards with `cmp <param>,<zeroreg>` instead of `test <param>,<param>`, and spills the zero from a register where we store an immediate. The early `return` in the failing branch ends the parameter's live range, freeing its incoming stack home for the address-taken out-param and freeing a register to carry the zero. The `if`-wraps-everything spelling overlaps the live ranges, so cl allocates a fresh slot (`push ecx`/`pop ecx` retail does not have). CInGameIcon::SetupSprite 0x99b10 64.21 -> 100.00 EXACT
- [A `u16*` scratch cursor blocks cl's IV elimination - retail walked the row with `u8*` + `Load16`](u16-cursor-blocks-the-induction-variable-elimination.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:loop cpp:cast | asm:lea asm:sub asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - a pixel-row loop stepping three buffers in lockstep is 8-40 instructions LONGER than retail and spills two cursors per iteration, while retail keeps ONE cursor register and reaches the others through a bias computed in the preheader (look for a `sub <reg>,<reg>` of two buffer bases there). cl 5.0 only coalesces induction variables of the same pointer type, so a `u16* sc = Scratch16()` mixed with the signature's `u8* dst` is never reduced; byte cursors plus the existing `Load16`/`Store16` union inlines relate all three and eliminate two. The freed registers then hold the loop-invariant LUT pointers retail keeps in registers, which is where the frame delta goes. DDrawShadeBlit 47 sites: ConvertRowDouble 62.78 -> 72.33, ConvertRowDoubleFwd 64.02 -> 71.78, ConvertRowFlip 65.99 -> 71.17
- [A shared block expanded TWICE in one caller must be a macro, not an inline function](inline-expanded-twice-costs-a-register.md) - c10 - cpp:inline cpp:call cpp:local | asm:push asm:pop | topic:codegen-idiom - after folding N open-coded copies into one `inline` helper, every caller that uses it ONCE stays byte-exact and every caller that uses it TWICE OR MORE drops ~30 points, with an extra `push ebp`/`pop ebp` and esi<->edi swaps. MSVC 5 shares one register plan across two inline expansions of a function but not across two macro expansions, and the rule is TRANSITIVE - making only the outer block a macro while its inner loop stays an inline function does not help. Four measured spellings of the act-name registrar: inline/inline, macro/inline and inline/textual all 3306 exact; macro/textual 3315. MSVC 5 has no __forceinline, which is why CGrunt's 19-block registrar was already a macro
- [A guard epilogue that runs BEFORE the callee-save pushes means the body was an inlined helper](inline-helper-region-defers-the-callee-save-push.md) - c9 - cpp:inline cpp:call cpp:return cpp:branch | asm:push asm:pop asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - a `jcc_sieve` POLARITY row reading `rets N -> N+1` where retail's FIRST `ret` sits BEFORE any `push esi`/`push edi` and those saves appear inside the body block in first-USE order (`push edi; push esi`, not the canonical prologue order). Both gate spellings are byte-identical, so the gate was never the variable; cl5 emits the callee-save pushes at the top of an INLINED REGION, so extract the post-guard body into a `static __inline` helper and `return Helper(...)`. The mechanical screen is `first ret index < first callee-save push index` on the target and the reverse on the base - it fired on exactly 3 of 62 POLARITY rows and closed all three. Pin the RVA on the METHOD, not the helper (an inlined helper emits no COMDAT and labels.py fails DROPPED N -> N-1). CChatBox::PlayFocusSound/PlayActivationSound 84.71 -> 100.00 EXACT each, CPlay::StepGridWalk 66.67 -> 100.00 EXACT
- [A `static` helper that every switch arm ends with is OUR invention - expand it](shared-tail-helper-is-our-invention-expand-it.md) - c10 - cpp:switch cpp:inline cpp:call | asm:call asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom - a factory/loader switch whose arms all finish with the same three statements folded into one `static` free function: /Ob1 does NOT auto-inline a plain `static`, so every arm pays a `call` and the tail's own callees vanish from the arm. `insn_seq --seq` shows one `- call <Helper>` per arm against `+ call <what the helper calls>`. Expand it at every site and DELETE it - retail writes the tail out per arm and lets tail merging share the suffix, which keeps each arm's `new`+ctor inline. `static __inline` is NOT the same fix (it took the same function to 0.00). CTileTriggerContainer::LoadElement 0x117800 71.27 -> 89.75
- [The EH funclet that calls an out-of-line destructor names the COMDAT's OWNER TU](eh-funclet-band-owns-the-inline-dtor-comdat.md) - c10 - cpp:dtor cpp:eh cpp:inline cpp:class | asm:call | topic:identity topic:eh - a `~CFoo()` whose only out-of-line callers are EH unwind thunks is an INLINE destructor, and its COMDAT belongs to the TU whose funclets call it - not to the class's own .cpp. `sema xref <dtor-rva>` gives `jmp 0x001dXXXX FUN_005dXXXX (via 1t)` addresses in one band; find the function whose prologue pushes a handler from that band and that TU is the owner. The whole COMDAT run moves with it (member dtors, `??_G`, `??_E` thunks); `build/gen/labels/<unit>.csv` verifies. Resolved five `@identity-TODO`s and took CChatBoxOwner::ProcessCheatInput 62.32 -> 76.01
- [`call _zvec::IndexToPtr` + an inline ctor loop IS `_zdvec::IndexToPtr`](zdvec-indextoptr-is-zvec-plus-ctor-loop.md) - c9 - cpp:inline cpp:container | asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - the target calls `?IndexToPtr@_zvec@@` where we call `?IndexToPtr@_zdvec@@`, and elsewhere expands the whole bounds-check/GrowTo/imul-m_stride body inline; both are ONE `*g_typeColl.GetNameRecord(key)` statement - cl expands the outer wrapper everywhere and picks per site whether to expand the inner `_zvec` call too. Writing the expansion out as a SECOND statement DCEs (its result is unused) and leaves the function 48 insns short. ArrivalRecycle 0x59230 68.46 -> 93.19
- [`if (!Ok()) { Report(); } return 1;` - not `if (Ok()) return 1; Report(); return 1;`](error-report-guard-falls-through-to-a-shared-return.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:switch | asm:jne asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom - every arm of a big command switch is TWO instructions longer than retail and the extra pair is always `movl $1,%eax; jmp <ret>` after an INVERTED branch. Retail's `jne` goes to ONE shared `mov eax,1; jmp ret` for the whole function because the arm's success path FALLS THROUGH to a single trailing return; an early `return 1` on success gives each arm its own copy. Two consecutive attempts are one `&&` of two negated calls. Mirror image: when the guarded block itself returns, retail keeps the report+epilogue inline and does NOT share - the retail branch polarity tells you which. CGruntzMgr::HandleCommand 0x862f0, 15 arms, 94.69 -> 97.34
- [`lea <reg>,[this+off]` + one register per field = a by-VALUE struct rvalue](rect-fill-through-a-base-register-is-a-byvalue-rvalue.md) - c10 - cpp:struct cpp:inline cpp:local | asm:lea asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - a member RECT filled with four `mov`s plateaus 10+ points below its neighbours: retail addresses the field through a `lea`d base and materializes each value in its OWN register (four `xor`s for four zeros), we emit disp32 stores reusing one zero register. Four scalar member assignments and an inline helper taking `RECT*` produce the SAME (wrong) shape - the pointer parameter folds into the displacement. Only a by-VALUE struct rvalue assigned to the member (`m_rect = CRect(...)` with MFC inlines on, `m_rect = MakeRect(...)` with them off) forces the base register and one register per field. CGrunt::LoadGruntTypeTable 0x4dd50, ~25 sites: +106 insns/47.67 -> -21 insns/58.50, frame 0x10 -> retail's 0xc
- [Two copies of the same struct: retail RELOADS every field, so it was a struct assignment](struct-copy-defeats-the-field-load-cse.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:struct cpp:expr | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - retail seeds two local RECTs from one pointer with EIGHT load/store pairs through TWO base registers where we emit four loads and eight stores. `RECT cur = *rect;` is a struct assignment: cl expands each one as its own block and does not CSE the loads across two expansions, so a `mov <reg>,<ptr>` of the source pointer appears between them. Field-by-field assignment is one basic block and cl folds the second read of every field away. Reading the reloads as regalloc is the trap. CFontConfig::DrawTextLines 0x22360 85.40 -> 89.51
- [A `{}` default constructor is wrong when the class owns 64-bit clock members: retail seeds every one](default-ctor-seeds-every-64bit-clock-member.md) - c10 - cpp:ctor cpp:class cpp:inline | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - a `new`-site that inlines `CFoo::CFoo()` is short by a MULTIPLE OF FOUR instructions and the missing rows are all `mov [esi+0xNN],edi` with edi==0 (retail's `cmp esi,edi` vs our `test esi,esi` is a consequence of that zero register, not a separate regalloc choice). Every Gruntz logic class with `i64`/`Clock64`/`CHazardTimer`/`CPairRecord` clock members seeds each of them to 0 in its default ctor; the store set is EXACTLY those members (CPathHazard seeds m_leg+m_strike and skips the i32 between them). The seeds land in DECLARATION order interleaved with the member ctor calls, so on a class that also has member ctors it must be a MEM-INIT LIST, not a body - and MSVC5 accepts a mem-init for a nested anonymous-union member (verified on CGrunt's 30 clocks). SerialObjectFactory 0xd2a0 86.74 -> 97.55, -108 insn -> +4
- [An extra /GX entry state between the last member ctor and the first real call is a CTOR BODY](ctor-body-is-its-own-eh-state.md) - c9 - cpp:ctor cpp:eh | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:eh - a `/GX` function whose local object emits N+1 entry states where the model only accounts for N destructible sub-objects; the extra transition lands after a call that looks like an ordinary member call on the fresh local
- [Two if/else arms retail emits in FULL: give each arm its own local](identical-arms-need-distinct-locals.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:local | asm:jmp asm:jne | topic:codegen-idiom - retail duplicates two `if/else` arms byte-for-byte (only a string immediate differs) while ours ends one arm with `push reg; jmp <middle of the other arm>` and is 30-50 instructions SHORT. cl5 cross-jumps arms whose remaining STATEMENTS are the same statements, not merely the same machine code - so which variables they name is load-bearing. Give each arm its own block-scope buffer/local and cl emits both in full (they still share one stack slot, so the frame is unchanged). Proof it is not machine-level tail merging: six byte-identical `return 0` blocks in the same function stay unmerged on both sides. Corollary when reading the target: a local sharing a slot with another local proves disjoint scopes, and the union WIDTH sizes it. CGruntzMgr::BuildLevelRezPath 0x93d40 78.67 -> 97.86
- [cl sinks identical TRAILING STATEMENTS out of sibling arms - a differing last statement blocks it](trailing-statement-blocks-arm-tail-sink.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:if cpp:local cpp:return | asm:jmp asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom topic:tail-merge - retail emits two if/else arms in FULL (~20i each) where the base leaves 4i stubs and a fat join; arm blocks SHORTER than the target's and the join LONGER by the same total, with ret counts EQUAL (so the exit-merge sieve misses it). cl 5.0 has TWO independent mechanisms: early IL statement-list SINKING out of sibling arms, and late machine-level cross-jump of instruction suffixes - only the latter is steered by goto fail/||. For the sink the knob is each arm's LAST STATEMENT: moving an existing arm-only statement from the head of an arm to its tail is enough. Micro-study: arms-fall-to-join 43 insns == arms-each-return 43 insns (so ending the function is IRRELEVANT), per-arm block-scope local 49 (partial - a pure alias gets CSEd back), one extra trailing statement 57 (full duplication). Retail proof it is not machine-level: StepEntranceReinit's B34/B40 share a 13-instruction byte-identical suffix that retail does NOT cross-jump. CGrunt::StepEntranceReinit 0x637a0 79.56 -> 83.37 -> 87.60 (join B41 exact) -> 90.18 (folding two hand-copied bounds-checked cell reads onto CMapMgr::CellFlagsAt)
- [In a `useLut` / per-bpp renderer, the LUT arm is retail's FALL-THROUGH](lut-arm-is-the-fallthrough-not-the-bpp-chain.md) - c9 - cpp:branch | asm:jcc asm:test | topic:codegen-idiom - the first ~17 blocks align exactly, then the recompile's first in-loop jcc goes ~25 blocks away while retail's goes to the very next test; every arm after is `!!`
- [Pinning a base constructor out-of-line to claim one RVA costs every derived ctor ~45 points](base-ctor-pinned-out-of-line-costs-every-derived-ctor.md) - c10 - cpp:ctor cpp:inline cpp:class | asm:call asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - a whole FAMILY of leaf constructors stuck at 40-55% where the base has one `call <base ctor>` and retail has 60-100 inlined instructions (two vptr stamps, the member stores, a guarded registration block). Retail EXPANDS a shared-header base ctor into every leaf, so moving that ctor into a .cpp to bind its retail RVA buys one row and taxes sixty-five. Tell which shape retail used by counting `sema xref` callers of the standalone body: a handful of SPECIFIC big functions (not the family) means inline-in-header. Measured on CUserLogic::CUserLogic 0x58cd0: ctors >=90% went 5 -> 45, tree fuzzy 86.19 -> 86.97, CTileTriggerSwitch 48.3 -> 100.0 EXACT
- [Two entities, continued: the split need not follow the class, and NESTED ctors need a TU split](nested-ctor-call-vs-expansion-is-a-tu-visibility-split.md) - c10 - cpp:ctor cpp:inline cpp:class | asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom topic:wall - what applying two-shapes-need-two-entities to a BIG base ctor adds. MSVC5 has no delegating ctors, so put the body in one inline member helper both ctors expand; what differs per entity goes in the MEM-INIT LIST, which is per-ctor. The split need not partition by class: CWwdGameObjectA is created at three sites, one expands the base and two call it, so it needs BOTH ctors and the `new`-site picks. The ctors NESTED inside the pinned body are a different lever again - making them inline makes cl expand them everywhere and their COMDATs vanish from every base obj (llvm-nm finds zero emitters, labels ratchet fires), so keep them declaration-only, home the out-of-line body in a TU that must CALL it, and give the one TU that expands them a `<Class>CtorInline.h`. A member sub-object is steerable from the mem-init list; a base sub-object or a free call inside the shared body is not. CUserLogic 0x58cd0 and CGameObject 0x15b390 both 0 -> 100.00 EXACT; CMotionState 0x136d0 took CGrunt::CGrunt 60.95 -> 90.40 and CProjectile::CProjectile 60.54 -> 86.23. tu_order spans count RVA() rows only, not RVA_COMPGEN pins; a cross-unit body move is a labels_manifest TRANSFER, ack with the unit named
- [Three retail oracles pin a class's layout - and they say our layouts are right](class-layout-has-three-retail-oracles.md) - c10 - cpp:class cpp:ctor cpp:member cpp:new | asm:push asm:lea asm:mov | topic:identity topic:wall - a diff row that looks like a member sits at the wrong offset (`lea ebx,[esi+0x18]` vs `lea edi,[esi+0x38]`), or the urge to move a member/shorten a class to close a diff. Retail states layout three ways and all three are sweepable: `push <n>; call ??2` pins sizeof (`gruntz.audit.alloc_size`), `lea ecx,[this+N]; call ??0Y` in a ctor/dtor pins each sub-object (`gruntz.audit.subobject_offsets`), and every `[this+N]` in a `?M@C@@..AE..` body pins the fields (`gruntz.audit.this_offsets`). Whole-image 2026-08-08: 430 alloc sites (253 classes) + 818 sub-object placements + 11332 field accesses, ZERO disagreements - so a plateau is almost never a layout bug. The masked diff aligns two `lea`s at DIFFERENT sub-objects: CProjectile's 0x18 is m_link from an inlined base ctor, 0x38 is m_motion, and retail's own ??0/??1 bodies name both. What the sweep DOES find is a misattributed METHOD (`IsAtSavedScreenPos` read +0x17c out of a 0x34-byte CUserLogic behind a static_cast<CGrunt*>(this))
- [EH states at every `new` are a CALLED base ctor, not a destructible local](new-site-eh-states-are-a-called-base-ctor.md) - c9 - cpp:eh cpp:ctor cpp:new | asm:call asm:mov | topic:wall topic:eh - `insn_count --eh` says retail has a /GX frame and we do not, in a big `new`-heavy builder, and retail's trylevels count 0,1,2,...,N one per `new`. - [The /GX EH frame is a SOURCE fact - sweep the tree for presence mismatches](eh-frame-presence-is-a-source-fact.md) - c9 - cpp:eh cpp:ctor cpp:local | asm:mov asm:push | topic:audit topic:eh - `/GX` is project-wide, so cl 5.0 emits `push -1`/`push <handler>`/`mov fs:[0],esp` iff the function owns something whose dtor must run on an unwind: a presence disagreement is a SOURCE fact, not a codegen preference. `python -m gruntz.audit.eh_frame` sweeps every scoring function both ways; `--states` is the wider secondary sieve (both sides framed, different NUMBER of `mov [esp+N],<n>` state stores). - [A store run retail emits in SOURCE order, that cl reorders no matter how you spell it, is a `volatile` receiver](unreorderable-store-run-is-a-volatile-receiver.md) - c10 - cpp:member cpp:struct cpp:local | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:wall-broken - a 10-15 instruction leaf setter with the correct instruction MULTISET and the correct size stuck in the low 70s because ONE store floats two slots from retail's position, and retail's order is exactly the order the assignments are written. cl canonicalizes the store order of independent same-object members, so source order is not a lever: 96 cells (24 permutations x i64-vs-lo/hi), 192 cells adding the union-member selections, and 7 receiver shapes (direct / `T&` / `T*` / `static inline` helper by ref or ptr) all collapse to the SAME two outputs - and perturbing the preceding function moves it 0.00, so it is not TU state either. - [An `RVA()` extent must include the function's own switch TABLES](rva-extent-must-include-switch-tables.md) - c10 - data:objdiff cpp:switch | asm:jmp | topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling - a function whose disasm is byte-identical (same instruction count, every branch displacement equal) is stuck below 100 and its base .text COMDAT is much longer than the RVA() size: MSVC5 emits a switch's jump table AND byte index table INSIDE the function's own COMDAT, so objdiff scores our table bytes against retail's NEXT function. Traps: a section can hold >1 function (bound at the next fn symbol) and RVA() is sometimes INDENTED in a namespace. SUPERSEDED DETECTOR: read RETAIL instead - from the claim end, 4-align, count DWORDs landing inside [rva, rva+size], then bytes < that count, and REPEAT (functions carry several switches); confirm each by requiring the body to literally contain the table's VA. That needs no base obj, so it also finds functions emitting NO table - where the short claim was INFLATING the score. Second sweep 2026-08-01: 65 confirmed / 0 rejected, 38 applied, MAX 81.73 -> 82.30; it refutes this entry's own `slop > 3 means real work` bound (_EngStr_RenderText 61.40 -> 90.55) and caught 3 multi-table fns (HandleCommand 3 tables +1551, 71.16 -> 94.70). - [`.bss` sections cap below 100% — objdiff infers COFF symbol sizes from the next symbol's offset](bss-symbol-size-inference-hole.md) — c9 — data:bss data:objdiff | topic:wall topic:scoring-artifact topic:tooling — a .bss section stalls at 99.99% and matched_data (all-or-nothing per SECTION) credits it 0; the residual is always ONE small int, the one cl put at section offset 0. NOT steerable: 3 cl probes prove the .bss layout is declaration-order INVARIANT (depends only on the object set + alignments). ddsurface = 197144 B = 70% of total_data, permanently capped. Do NOT candidate-shape .bss (no bytes exist -> 100% by construction for ANY globals = vacuous) and do NOT add filler globals. Fix is upstream (delinker emits sizes / score bss by declared type), not in src/
- [ILT: retail is linked `/INCREMENTAL`, so a vtable slot holds a `jmp` thunk, not the body](ilt-thunk-indirection.md) — c10 — asm:jmp | topic:delinker topic:scoring-artifact topic:reloc-fidelity — a delinked vtable slot names an unrelated class (`~CTriggerMgr`, `GroupAllScored`, `SetupImage`) or `thunk_FUN_...`, and its `.rdata` can never reach 100%. link.exe /INCREMENTAL routes function-ADDRESS refs through a 5-byte `jmp rel32` band at the start of .text (GRUNTZ: rva 0x1005..0x44a8, 2695 entries, `cc` pad both ends). `cl` cannot name a thunk that does not exist until link, so EVERY DIR32 in the original obj named the BODY - delinking must hop through the thunk (nix/patches/vostok-ilt-thunk-resolution.patch). Corollary: NEVER pin `RVA_COMPGEN`/`RVA()` to a vtable slot's stored VALUE - that pins the symbol onto a 5-byte jmp (14 CUserLogic virtuals did exactly this; real bodies are the 0x87d0..0x89f0 cluster). Reverse audit: invented helpers may chase through one ILT entry to an existing function, into an internal shared tail that calls a real member, or through a `call;ret` wrapper whose consumed EAX proves the member return type (`CueVisible`/`GruntPointVisible`/`BoardTest` -> `PointInBounds`; `EntranceApplyFrame` -> `ApplyLookupSprite`; `GruntTileSwitchImpl` -> `StepArrivalDrop`). A callee-cleaned `ret N` does not by itself prove `__stdcall`: ECX use and caller receiver setup exposed `0x11b7c0` as a five-argument member overload. Verify every call site independently: one fake name may hide different retail targets or an entire missing branch (`ApplyBox`). Trap: tiny default bodies draw FID LOW false-positives (`__fpclear` claimed at 4 RVAs at once)

- [The two-way select whose low arm has NO `jmp`: the default is hoisted INTO the destination](default-hoists-into-destination-no-jmp.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:local cpp:ternary | asm:mov asm:jmp asm:jle | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - retail's select is `mov <dst>,<default> / cmp / jcc J / mov <dst>,[other] / J:` with the low arm a bare fall-through, and the recompile emits the textbook two-arm `jcc L1 / .. / jmp J / L1: .. / J:` (2 extra instructions per site + a different join colouring). - [`return <callee result>` - the `int` return with NO constant anywhere to give it away](void-return-collapses-the-guard-ret.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:return | asm:ret asm:jne asm:test | topic:codegen-idiom - retail spells each null-guard `test eax,eax / jne <+1> / ret` - a one-byte `ret` the guard jumps OVER - where the base has `je <shared epilogue>`; the base has FEWER `ret`s and NO constant appears on either side, so an `int` function disassembles exactly like a `void` one and only the ret COUNT gives it away. The third subcase of the void-vs-int family: `void-vs-bool` has a `mov eax,1` tell, `identical-return-epilogue-tailmerge` is the all-`return 0` wall, this one returns the CALLEE'S result so nothing is materialised. Declared `int` with `return 0;` guards the same source is byte-exact - eax already holds the just-tested NULL, so no `xor` appears and the guard block stops being identical to the success block. Sweep: `python -m gruntz.audit.void_return_type`. SaveFrontBufferShotImpl 0x114f00 87.69 -> 100, EngStr_DrawText 0x115440 / ShowHudMessage 0x1154b0 / ShowHudMessageAlt 0x115520 94.29 -> 100 EXACT each (all three had been parked with four guard spellings already tried)

- [Positive-form gate (`if (ready){body}`) is what makes cl shrink-wrap a callee-save push](positive-gate-enables-shrink-wrap.md) — c9 — cpp:branch cpp:return cpp:local | asm:push asm:pop asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom — retail's prologue saves FEWER callee-saved regs and emits the missing `push esi`/`push edi` after a ready/null gate, its early exit jumping BELOW the matching `pop` (= the push is provably conditional); the recompile's early-return spelling saves them all up front. Rewrite the gate positively (`if(ready){whole body}` + one trailing `return 0`) instead of `if(!ready) return;` — CWarlord::BuildFortSplashParticles 93.1→98.1%, AdvanceMovingAnim 90.3→95.9%. STEERABLE half of shrink-wrapped-callee-save-push.md (its INVERSE direction is still a wall). ALSO decides BLOCK LAYOUT: an early-return miss handler is laid out INLINE right after its test with its own epilogue, where retail parks it PAST the success return so it falls into the shared early-exit epilogue the other returns tail-merge into (CInGameText::Update @0x997c0 79.46 -> 93.83, and the `&= ~1` switches to retail's `and [mem],imm8` RMW on its own; +y-before-x coord loads and taking g_gameReg into a local AFTER them -> 96.59). MECHANICAL TEST = COUNT THE `ret`s: apply only when base has MORE epilogues than retail (`b_ret > t_ret`); equal counts mean the gate spelling is already right and applying it REGRESSES (CenterScrollA/B 87.9->72.8, UpdateGruntStatus 94.5->77.1, reverted). Past ~4 gates use the `goto fail;` + one bottom `fail: return 0;` spelling (hoist declarations - cl5 C2362 on a goto skipping an initialisation): CaptureSystemPalette 68.4->100 EXACT, CSBI_GruntMachine::Render 88.6->100, CheckSortOrder 78.7->100, HeapCheckDump 96.7->100, PlaceCursorTarget 70.9->97.8, CFaderShape::ApplyInit 40.2->72.6, SoundDevice::CreateBuffer 35.6->53.5, CChatBoxOwner::HitTest 38.1->63.1. Does NOT apply when the base is missing retail's /GX frame, nor to a /GX function whose shared exit runs a destructor (BuildGameMenu 59.0->49.4: cl drops the new-expression cleanup frame). SECOND SWEEP 2026-07-27 resolves `b_ret>t_ret` into FOUR distinct spellings (20 more fns moved, 1 EXACT): (1) SEARCH LOOP - hoist the guard, `if(c){do{...}while(c);}`; a plain for/while duplicates the miss epilogue as the loop fall-through where retail branches out and takes an unconditional back-edge (MgrListFind 73.2->89.5, FindStartPointAt 83.2->93.5, CellHitTest 71.2->80.7, FindGruntAt 74.8->79.4 - three of those were filed as regalloc walls); (2) WNDPROC - `break` not `return 0` in a case arm merges it with the switch DEFAULT, parking that block at the bottom and inverting the ladder's last compare (PreDispatchMessage 77.6->98.2, LevelPreviewDlgProc 80.4->85.2, GameOptionsDlgProc 96.1->97.6) - and the dispatch must actually BE a switch, chained `if(msg==X)` is not (ButeAttributezDlgProc needed both: exits alone 27.9->14.9, +switches 100.00 EXACT); (3) MANY GATES - `goto fail` still puts the fail block BEFORE the success tail (2 epilogues); carrying the value (`i32 ok=0; ... ok=1; done: return ok;`) is what gives ONE (LoadGameAssetNamespaces 86.2->88.5) but is NOT universal (same edit on LevelPreviewDlgProc 85.2->79.8, reverted); (4) TWO-ARM STORE at a tail - retail selects the slot pointer and stores once (zPTree::Insert 53.4->60.5, CProjActMap::Insert 58.2->63.6, CWarpStoneFly::Tick 79.4->80.5). Plus: a mid-fn guard duplicating a LATER shared exit, written positively (FocusNext/FocusPrev 77.1->97.2 each, WaitForOtherPlayers 75.0->80.5, VerifyCustomLevel 6.2->23.5, FindProcessByName 86.1->93.5, ActivateSlot 71.1->83.6, CGruntVoice::Update 73.2->85.8, WapUncompress 85.9->87.6, MoveHandlerA 65.0->70.0 - that last one a real control-flow bug the count exposed). Bound re-confirmed: `b_ret>t_ret` can also mean the base is missing a whole inlined /GX construction (DispatchKeyed2C/30/34/38) - screen those out by the EH prologue the base lacks INVERSE MEASURED 2026-08-01: the rule is NOT symmetric - SoundDevice::FreeSamples 0x136ed0 has retail NOT shrink-wrapping while cl does, from the early-return form on BOTH sides; its body is byte-identical and the positive form COSTS 5 points (77.31 -> 72.13, a result variable 71.39; see the measured matrix in the pattern document). Its TU sibling DSoundCloneInst::GetItem 0x135d70 needs the OPPOSITE decision from the same guard shape with all 4 spellings tied at 90.31. SCREEN FIRST: only reach for the positive form when retail's early exit lands BELOW a `pop`; if it pops everything, the gate is not the variable

- [`#include <new>` silently deletes a /GX unwind frame - use `<new.h>`](nothrow-new-header-kills-gx-frame.md) - c10 - cpp:include cpp:dtor cpp:eh cpp:new | asm:push asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:eh - a /GX destructor is missing retail's WHOLE EH prologue (`push -1 / push handler / mov fs:0,esp / push ecx / mov [esp+4],this / mov [esp+0x10],0`) and no body spelling brings it back. The C++ `<new>` declares `operator delete(void*) throw()`; a nothrow delete leaves the region with no throw point, so cl drops the base-subobject unwind state AND the frame. `<new.h>` supplies placement new WITHOUT the exception spec. ??1CRezBufferObject 46.26 -> 100.00 EXACT on the include alone; six body spellings all scored an identical 46% while `<new>` was in. SCOPE (swept all 11 remaining `<new>` TUs 2026-08-01): 10/11 are exact NO-OPS - the spec only decides the unwind state when the delete is the region's SOLE throw point, i.e. a minimal dtor whose whole body is `if (m_p) delete m_p;` in a /GX unit. Do NOT sweep: `<new.h>` is smaller and RENUMBERS every `$S<n>` local-static COMDAT in the TU
- [The TU's file-scope DECLARATION COUNT picks the register scheme - map the window, then land a real `#include`](declaration-count-window-steers-regalloc.md) - c10 - cpp:include cpp:class | asm:mov asm:lea asm:push | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc topic:wall - a block-exact, selection-exact function where every register is rotated (eax<->edx, one extra callee-saved push, `mov eax,[disp32]` vs `mov edx,[disp32]`) and NOTHING inside the function moves a byte - hand matrices, the 300-cell rename forest and the AST trees all score identically. Quantitative form of string-h-intrinsics-reallocate-the-tu: sweep N throwaway prototypes above the first project include and READ THE WINDOW. CRezImage::FillRectAt 0x176da0 is period 16 with an 8-wide window (N mod 16 in 8..15 => 100.00 size 75, else 66.44 size 77, verified N=0..24), CImage::BlitShadeNorm 0x154270 is 100.00 only at N=9,10,11,16 - the window is FUNCTION-SPECIFIC. A class with inline member BODIES is the densest carrier (the `tu_state_member` island family flips these 5/5 where data-member classes and free functions do nothing). Only landable form is a REAL missing #include, preferably one owning a symbol the TU already uses: <Rez/FrameClock.h> in GruntPowerupSprite.cpp (g_engineFrameDelta) 89.82 -> 100.00 EXACT, <Gruntz/StatusBarMgr.h> in SBI_Image.cpp (CStatusBarMgr param) 74.07 -> 100.00 EXACT. - [An include's POSITION picks a function's register scheme (cl 5.0)](string-h-intrinsics-reallocate-the-tu.md) - c9 - cpp:include | asm:mov asm:reg | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc topic:wall - cl 5.0 picks a function's register assignment partly from a TU-cumulative count of file-scope DECLARATIONS parsed so far. Declarations above a function rotate it; declarations BETWEEN two functions rotate only the second. Blowfish_encipher/decipher are near-twins that retail compiled into different schemes: `<memory.h>` above both gives 100.00/61.64, `<string.h>` above both 60.41/100.00, `<string.h>` BETWEEN them 100.00/100.00 (whole unit exact). The window is one declaration wide (N=4 dummy prototypes hits it, N=3 and N=5 miss). NOT the intrinsic set - MSVC5 STRING.H has no `#pragma intrinsic`. NOT expression spelling - 8 equal spellings of the round macro are byte-identical. Bodies do not count, only declarations. When two near-twins in one TU cannot both be exact, put the declarations between them

- [cl5 never inlines a helper that owns an EH region - spell `new X(...)` at every site](eh-region-helper-never-inlines.md) - c9 - cpp:new cpp:eh cpp:inline cpp:ctor | asm:push asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - sibling functions all MISSING retail's whole /GX prologue and emitting one `call <helper>` where retail expands `push <sizeof>; call ??2@YAPAXI@Z` + the full inline ctor chain. A `new`-expression on a class with a destructible sub-object owns an EH region (state var 0/1/-1 + __except_list frame) and cl 5.0 REFUSES to inline any function containing one - `static inline` is ignored and there is no __forceinline. So retail's source spelled the allocate-and-construct block out at every site; paste it back into each body and give the class a real INLINE ctor (store order [base ctor][member sub-object ctors][derived vptr stamp][derived body]; args materialize right-to-left, so the FIRST load is the RIGHTMOST parameter). Use `map.SetAt(k,v)` not `map[k]=v` when retail loads the value BEFORE the container call. CDDrawWorkerRegistry DispatchKeyed38/34/30/2C + InstallTree 38/40/51 -> 100 EXACT (unit 65.5 -> 97.6); the intermediate helper-holding-the-new measured 17%, the diagnostic.

- [cl 5.0 DROPS an inlined destructor and its `return` unless the function is the compiland's FIRST optimized one](ob1-budget-drops-the-inlined-dtor-and-the-return.md) - c10 - cpp:dtor cpp:eh cpp:inline cpp:switch | asm:jmp | topic:wall topic:codegen-bug - a `/GX` function with a destructible local whose inline dtor is a switch on a member the ctor just set to a constant: the `return` inside that scope emits ONLY `mov __$EHRec$[esp+N],-1` and FALLS THROUGH into the next statement - WRONG CODE, no `operator delete`, no jump to the epilogue. Confirmed in cl's own `/FAs` listing. The big `--blocks --diff` block-count divergence (butemgr SetPoint 70 vs 52) IS this defect, not a separate switch-arm problem: retail's two inlined `CopyValue` jump tables share ONE arm set whose tail carries the folded dtor, and when the defect fires cl emits two arm sets and loses the tail. Census by counting in-body `??3@YAXPAX@Z` (skip the `$T..[ebp-4]` unwind funclets): butemgr's SetInt/SetDword/SetFloat/SetDouble have 3-4, SetRect/SetPoint/SetVector/SetRange have ZERO. TRIGGER isolated: any preceding OPTIMIZED definition in the compiland - `#pragma optimize("",off)` around that predecessor keeps the victim clean, data-only and declaration-only predecessors are inert, and content placed AFTER is inert. Binary, not graded (1 == 20 predecessors). Nothing reaches it: /Zm50..1000, /Ob1, /Ob2, /G3, /G5, /Gy, /Gf, /Ot, /Os, /Oy, /Op, /Gs are byte-identical; /Oa,/Ow only reschedule (and crater `Parse`); braces, an if/else chain, a `goto` chain, a TEMPORARY `CopyValue(&CButeValue(t,v))` and a consumed return value all still drop the branch; moving the ctors out of the class body is byte-identical. The ONE pragma that is not inert is `inline_depth`: depth 0 scoped to the victim, or depth 1 above the whole include block, both restore the destructor (see msvc5-inline-depth-zero-is-the-only-live-lever) - that is the workaround now in `src/Bute/ButeMgr.cpp`, not retail's shape

Also re-measured: cl expands 6 of 7 alone AND in the full TU, not 7 alone. Keep the one authentic inline definition and record the census in the `@early-stop`.

Census the effect with the reloc multiset (`??2@YAPAXI@Z` per caller = inlined allocating ctors, `??0X` = out-of-line ones). - [The /Ob1 expansion count is set by the callee's FRONT-END size - transcribing cl's own fold under-costs it](inline-callee-frontend-cost-drives-ob1-budget.md) - c10 - cpp:inline cpp:ctor cpp:dtor cpp:switch | asm:call asm:jmp | topic:codegen-idiom - SUPERSEDES the "no visibility choice reaches the middle" verdict. Hold visibility fixed and add front-end cost to the inline callee and the expansion count walks down one site at a time (monotone over 0-20 dead statements, callee COMDAT byte-exact throughout). The real cost was missing because the header had transcribed cl's OWN FOLD: retail's 9-entry jump table at 0x1721b4 hits 4 arm bodies, and ~CButeValue had been written as 4 merged arms with the payload types erased to i32*/u32*. One typed `delete` per case took butemgr 74.48 -> 85.32 and ~CButeValue 99.30 -> 100. Retail's arms also prove the four payload structs have TRIVIAL destructors (only the CString arm null-tests) - adding an empty ~T() scores HIGHER on the nine Set<T> and is refuted by the dtor's own bytes. **2026-08-08:** the lever is proven to be the CTOR alone - the dtor/CopyValue levers saturate and the CALLER's own size INVERTS the decision (more caller code = more expansions). Residue is 3 IR nodes; weights table in the doc.
- [`push eax` straight after a ctor call means the source wrote `&Temporary()`](address-of-temporary-reuses-ctor-return.md) - c9 - cpp:ctor cpp:temporary cpp:inline | asm:call asm:lea | topic:codegen-idiom - A __thiscall ctor returns `this` in eax and cl 5 only USES that return value for a TEMPORARY; for a named local it re-`lea`s the address. So `hit->CopyValue(&CButeValue(BUTE_POINT, val))` (MSVC C4238 rvalue-as-lvalue), not `CButeValue box(...); ...(&box)`. A lifetime-extended `const&` is NOT equivalent (it moves /Ob1 the other way). Took the nine `CButeMgr::Set<T>` 784.52 -> 823.59 and killed one surplus /Ob1 expansion in the four scalar ones. Same idiom on aggregates: a stack image built right before `operator new` + a block copy is a temporary (`new CButeValue(BUTE_VECTOR, &ButeDoubleVector(x,y,z))`), NOT a field-wise ctor overload - four such overloads had been invented; deleting them took ParseAttributeFile 46.00 -> 49.18. Cost: clang errors on `&Temporary()`, so the three clang flag sites pass -Wno-address-of-temporary (byte-neutral).
- [A tiny helper sitting immediately AFTER each of its callers is that class's CONSTRUCTOR](helper-after-each-caller-is-that-classs-ctor.md) - c9 - cpp:ctor cpp:inline cpp:class | asm:call asm:new | topic:codegen-idiom topic:identity - a family of near-identical 0x31-byte functions, one per Set<Type> caller, each starting 8 bytes past the end of that caller; the call is guarded by `test eax,eax; je` around a preceding operator new and its eax return is immediately pushed. That is `new T(args)` / `T local(args)`, so the helper is ??0Class@@, not a Set* API. The Bute family had eight phantom CButeValue::SetXxx methods on those RVAs (Int/Dword/Float rotated by one slot); pinning the real ctors is byte-neutral. Spelling the ctor body `pValue = new i32(v)` rather than the expanded if/else also matters: the null arm becomes `xor eax,eax; mov [esi+4],eax` (the new-expression's live result register) instead of an immediate store, and the smaller IL moved all nine CButeMgr::Set* callers 5-19 points

- [Enum domains: MSVC5 sizes every enum as `int`, but `i32` -> enum is BYTE-NEUTRAL](enum-domains.md) - c10 - cpp:enum cpp:switch | asm:mov asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - measured on VC5.0 SP3: `enum class` and `enum E : u8` do NOT parse (C2236/C2059) and `sizeof(enum)` is ALWAYS 4, so a 1-byte field can never be enum-typed; but retyping a member/param/return/switch-key from `i32` to a real enum and naming the literals leaves `.text` byte-identical - only a signature's mangling moves (`H` -> `W4Kind@@`). An OPAQUE `enum Kind;` forward declaration compiles, gives the SAME mangling and `.text`, and types a header without including the domain's definition - the escape hatch from the include-adding regalloc butterfly. Real enums also make `cl` itself reject wrong-domain arguments (C2664) and raw-int assignment (C2440)

- [`mov [this+N],<zero reg>` where retail RE-READS the member it just tested against NULL](branch-equality-propagated-into-the-guarded-store.md) - c8 - cpp:if cpp:member cpp:assign | asm:cmp asm:mov | topic:wall topic:regalloc - `if (p->f == NULL) { m_x = p->f; }` - after `cmp [p+N],<zeroreg> / jne` our cl5 records the equality and stores the zero register, retail re-loads `[p+N]` inside the branch though it also has a zero in a register. Exactly one instruction short; a local for the value, a local for the receiver, `!p->f`, and an inline helper taking the receiver all fail to move it (re-spelling through the other member holding the same pointer defeats it but overshoots by a pointer load). Expensive indirectly: the extra use of 0 can tip cl into homing a callee-saved zero register retail lacks (`push ebx / xor ebx,ebx`), which is what pins CFrontCandyAni 0xacf40 at 49.3. Also CAniCycle 0xaad20, CEyeCandy 0xac620
- [`cmp reg,reg` where ours has `cmp [mem],reg`: the comparison's LEFT operand was a NAMED TEMPORARY](compare-lhs-materialized-before-its-call.md) - c9 - cpp:expr cpp:local cpp:call cpp:if | asm:cmp asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - `x->field >= f(...)` gives `call f / cmp [reg+disp],eax` in ours and `mov edi,[reg+disp] / call f / cmp edi,eax` in retail, so the function is one instruction SHORT per comparison and the masked diff shows only what looks like an operand-mode choice. cl5's operand evaluation order is not fixed by the source, but a named temporary IS a sequenced statement - hoist each comparison's LHS. The second arm of a `||` has no statement position, so it needs `(t = expr) >= f(...)`. Seven candy/animation ctors share the big-act guard verbatim: CFrontCandy 95.15->99.83, CBehindCandy 92.47->99.83, CDoNothing 94.68->99.53, CSimpleAnimation 95.15->98.72, CEyeCandy 94.51->97.85, CEyeCandyAni 93.58->96.60
- [Count the relocation MULTISET before you read its ordered diff](reloc-multiset-before-reloc-sequence.md) - c10 - topic:method topic:triage topic:mis-model | asm:call | cpp:call - `insn_seq --seq` must produce an ALIGNMENT, so a wrong callee is reported as a MOVE: it pairs our extra `A` against retail's extra `B` and narrates block placement, which no amount of statement reordering can fix. A `collections.Counter` over each side's relocation symbols is order-free and answers the stronger question - does retail even call this? Read the rows as: (1) `tgt=0`/`base=0` = a callee that exists on one side only (wrong callee, or a whole statement missing) - fix first; (2) a complementary pair summing equal = a rename/overload swap, which names the replacement for free; (3) off-by-one on a repeated callee = a cross-jumped or duplicated arm; (4) only then the ordered diff. CPlay::Render had been read as pure block placement for sessions; the multiset showed LoadDestructButtonSprite tgt=0/base=3 against LoadMainStatusBarSprite tgt=4/base=1 and FilterList2 tgt=2/base=4 against StepGridWalk tgt=3/base=1, and the surplus `_g_frameDelta` fell out of the same swap (the wrong callee took an argument the right one does not). Measured: CPlay::Render 71.84 -> 76.06, CBattlezMapConfig::ResolveArrival 56.73 -> 82.34, CGrunt::UpdateArrival's tail (SetEntrancePos where retail calls CTriggerMgr::ApplyTriggerA)
- [The ordered RELOC SEQUENCE names the missing STATEMENT in a -200-instruction function](reloc-sequence-diff-names-the-missing-statement.md) - c10 - topic:method topic:triage | asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - `python -m gruntz.audit.insn_seq <rva>`: keep only the instructions carrying a relocation, as (mnemonic, symbol) pairs in program order, and difflib the two sides. That list IS the function's statements and it survives regalloc and block scheduling. A long unbroken `+` run = a block retail has and you do not (reconstruct it, do not tune); a `-`/`+` pair on one row = a wrong callee or global; a group moved as a unit = block PLACEMENT, i.e. a source-shape idiom. Per-SYMBOL counts find a deleted switch arm faster than the diff. Two false alarms: the two objs can NAME the same address differently (pooled 1-char literals get a neighbour's name - resolve the ADDRESS), and `$T...` vs a named double is the FP constant pool. Measured: TryTeleportToCell 42.21 -> 92.99, AdvanceMotion 64.14 -> 79.03, ScanNearestTarget 51.30 -> 65.71
- [A jump-table switch lays its arms out in SOURCE order - read the tables to recover it](switch-arm-layout-order-is-source-order.md) - c10 - cpp:switch topic:codegen-idiom topic:method | asm:jmp - Unpack the `movb <idx>` / `jmpl *<jt>(,%ecx,4)` tables straight out of the image; sorting the DISTINCT arm targets by address gives retail's `case` order, because cl emits case bodies in source order, not value order. LoadTileArrivalFx's tables said SHOVEL/GAUNTLETZ/GOOBER/SPY/BRICK/TOOB and its nested brick switch said RED/GOLD/BLUE/BLACK - both different from what we had guessed. Also: MSVC5 emits ONE unique-target entry per CONTIGUOUS run of case values, so two adjacent entries holding the same address is a normal `case a: case b: case d:` group, NOT two bodies that got merged - splitting them emits the body twice. Measured 45.15 -> 61.67
- [The inline-strcmp FORM tells you a callee-saved register got spilled](inline-strcmp-form-reads-out-a-spilled-register.md) - c9 - topic:triage topic:wall | asm:cmp asm:mov asm:sub-esp | cpp:locals - MSVC5's /Oi inline strcmp has a 2-register form (`mov bl,[eax]` / `cmp bl,[esi]`, 4 movb) and a 3-register form (both operands loaded, 6 movb). In a strcmp if/else ladder the extra 2 movb per comparison IS the diff - 102 of CInGameIcon's +128. Group the base obj's `movb` operand shapes to spot it. The form is only a READOUT: reconcile the prologue instead - retail took one MORE callee-saved register and four FEWER stack bytes, having promoted the constant zero into ebx, which freed a byte register for every strcmp. Do not hand-tune the ladder
- [cl TAIL-MERGES repeated inline-helper expansions; retail keeps them apart](tail-merged-inline-helper-collapses-repeat-cues.md) - c8 - topic:wall topic:codegen-idiom | asm:call asm:jmp - `insn_seq` per-symbol counts show a helper's INTERIOR call short by N while every call site matches: cl 5.0 cross-jumps the identical block tails of N inlined expansions onto one shared call. Retail additionally expanded LeafCue::PlayIfElapsed's BODY at one of the four sites, which only happens if that member was defined IN the class body - the (unattempted, cross-TU) source lever. - [A gate that always `return`s lets cl DELETE a later re-test of the SAME storage](always-returning-gate-dce-kills-a-later-switch-arm.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:switch cpp:dce | asm:cmp asm:jne | topic:codegen-idiom - a whole `case` arm is ABSENT from the base obj (the dispatch falls straight into the arm's failure tail) and the reloc-name histogram shows one complete cluster of callees each short by exactly one. cl5 /O2 propagates "this storage is 0" out of a guard all of whose exits `return`, so a later `if (m_x != 0)` on the same member is folded away. Retail emits both because the GATE reads a local (one cached load, several `cmp reg,0`) while the arm re-reads the MEMBER - cl does not propagate across the two storage kinds. - [A per-function MNEMONIC HISTOGRAM (base vs target) names the wrong idiom in one shot](mnemonic-histogram-diff-finds-the-wrong-idiom.md) - c9 - topic:method topic:triage | asm:cdq asm:or asm:fidiv | topic:codegen-idiom - when the instruction COUNT says a bug exists but the stream is too long to read, count each mnemonic per function on both sides and print only the differing opcodes. A wrong source idiom appears as a PAIR of rows - one family we over-emit, one we under-emit: `cltd 0->4 / sarl 8->4` = hand-rolled abs vs the intrinsic; `orw 16->8 / orl 0->11` = a `static_cast<u16>` forcing a 16-bit OR chain; `shll 10->0 / movb 11->0 / orb 5->0` = we inline an LCG retail CALLS (`rand()` vs the inline `GameRand()`); `retl 16->12 / popl 64->48` = four early returns retail tail-merged. Calibrate on a 100% function (histogram must be IDENTICAL); ignore `addb/aas/hlt/sldtw/lretl` on the target side - that is the in-.text jump table disassembled as garbage. Four of five wins in one 40-75 batch came from this

- [Empty `break` switch arms de-dup BEFORE the jump-table decision - spell them `return <v>`](switch-empty-arms-dedup-before-jumptable.md) - c9 - cpp:switch cpp:return | asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - retail has a dense `add eax,-lo / cmp / ja / jmp [eax*4+T]` over a contiguous range where only 2-3 arms do anything, ours emits a `sub/je - dec/je - sub/jne` ladder over just the active values. cl5 collapses identical (empty `break`) arms BEFORE the density test, so 6 labels with 4 empty ones are already a 2-label switch; give each inactive arm its OWN `return <same value>;` and the table comes out (its 6 entries share 3 targets - the merge happens after). NOT just EMPTY arms - the fold is on body EQUALITY: a RUN of fall-through labels sharing one body is already one arm (winapi_02c140_IntersectRect_PtInRect 58.26 -> 63.71; its retail table is 14 entries ALL on 0x2c32e, which nothing but 14 separate arms can produce), and two ACTIVE arms spelled the same way merge too - retail keeps them apart as the same logic spelled two ways, readable off branch polarity (`jne` forward into the shared `mov eax,1` = `if (v) break; return 0;`; the arm just ABOVE that tail inverts to `je` = `if (!v) return 0; break;`) - SerializeApplyA 57.31 -> 100 EXACT, SerializeApplyB 63.26 -> 100 EXACT, CAniAdvanceCursor::Find 80 -> 100 EXACT, all three filed as unfoldable. - [/Od `[ebp-N]` slots are ordered by the local's NAME (hash), not decl position - SOLVED](od-local-slot-ordering.md) - c9 - cpp:local | asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - /Od fn recompiles byte-identical in mnemonics/operands/frame-size but the locals map to a permuted set of the same stack slots (~85% byte / 99.5% fuzzy). - [One extra branch in a loop: cl rotates the loop CONDITION, not the first in-body `break`](loop-rotates-on-the-CONDITION-not-the-first-break.md) - c9 - cpp:loop cpp:branch | asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - `--branches --diff` says base has exactly ONE more conditional branch, and it duplicates a test that also sits at the top of the loop; retail's back-edge jumps to that top test, ours jumps past it into the body. A two-exit loop (`while (A) { if (B) break; ... }`) has two semantically identical spellings and cl5 rotates only the CONDITION - peeling it as the entry guard and making the bottom test the back-edge - so whichever test you make the condition is the one that gets DUPLICATED, and the one you leave as a `break` becomes the loop head the back-edge lands on. Read it off `--blocks --lite --target`: the test inside the back-edge block IS the condition. HAND-ROTATING DOES NOT WORK (`if (A) do { if (B) break; ... } while (A);` still duplicates B) - only promoting the right test to the condition moves it, which is also why the earlier "while/for(;;)/do-while are byte-identical" negative is consistent (they all keep the same test as the condition). - [Two TUs deriving the SAME string RVA proves nothing - retail POOLED its literals](pooled-string-literals-not-tu-evidence.md) - c10 - topic:scoring-artifact topic:tu-partition | cpp:global - a `static const char s_x[] = "..."` in two or more .cpp files pairs to ONE retail address, read as "these files are slices of one .obj". Proof: retail .data holds 2178 distinct strings and only 2 have ANY duplicate; all literals sit in one block at 0x208000..0x21c000 while ordinary globals live at 0x244000+; and "Grunt" @0x20a9ec is referenced from 28 sites spanning 0xa4b5 (gameobjectfactory) to 0xd1da9 (playercommandstep). the delink manifest itself names 0x20a9ec `??_C@_05DNOF@Grunt?$AA@` and attributes it to FOUR objects (2289 of 3482 rows are ??_C@ string COMDATs; 372 rvas have >1 owner), and data_tu_order exempts them from the band invariant. Corollary: hoisting a pooled literal into `static const char s_x[]` turns it into an ORDINARY single-owner `_s_x$S<n>` array that can no longer be bound and DOES form a band - spell it inline. REAL partition evidence is ORDINARY data interleaving at dword granularity + .text containment (that pair proved six .cpp files were one rasterizer object, merged 2026-07-29)

- [Two locals in each other's `[esp+N]` slots = their DECLARATIONS are in the wrong order](local-slot-order-is-declaration-order.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:scope | asm:lea asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:frame - the frame size AGREES and every instruction pairs, but `&key` sits at `[esp+0x1c]` where retail has it at `[esp+0x20]` and `&found` mirrors it. cl5 hands scalar locals slots in DECLARATION order, ascending address (an inner-scope local declared later still comes later), so the slot order reads back the source's declaration order directly - a swapped pair is a source bug, not allocator noise. Corollary: an initialiser retail emits INSIDE a branch must be a plain assignment there, not a declaration-with-initialiser at the outer scope. CExitTrigger::SerializeMove 0x3f040 96.65 -> 98.95 (that plus an `i32 key = 0` retail never stores)
- [A switch's default arm that loads a PARAMETER slot for no reason: an uninitialised local](uninit-local-blocks-the-default-arm-jump-thread.md) - c8 - cpp:switch cpp:local | asm:mov asm:jmp asm:ja | topic:codegen-idiom topic:frame - retail's range check sends the default edge STRAIGHT to the shared `return` and drops the pre-switch `tag = -1` seeds as dead; ours keeps them (`or esi,-1` / `xor eax,eax`) and grows a default block whose only instruction reads a parameter's stack slot. That garbage load is cl5 materialising an UNINITIALISED local so the join has a value, and the non-empty block is what blocks the thread - give the local an initialiser. Same function: an otherwise byte-identical body with `sub esp,0x20` vs retail `0x24` means the buffer is one byte longer than the API cap it is passed (`char name[0x21]`), and inverting the guard to an early `return 0` un-shares retail's single epilogue (98.99 -> 83.45). DrawSaveGameMenu 0xe3f40 98.69 -> 98.99
- [A Win32 `void**` out-param takes the MEMBER's address - a local plus a copy costs the copy](void-outparam-is-the-member-address.md) - c10 - cpp:call cpp:member cpp:cast | asm:lea asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:mis-model - the pushed `ppvBits`/`ppvObject` is `[this+N]` (`lea ecx,[esi+0x42c]`) with NO `mov [esi+0x42c],reg` after the call; only the return is stored. Pass `PtrOut(&m_member)` (include/ComOutRef.h - the typed T**/void** union, no cast at the site, cast ratchet unmoved). A plain `reinterpret_cast<void**>` also reaches 100 but costs one ratchet unit per site, and a NAMED union local costs 4.6% because cl gives the union a frame slot. CRezImage::DecodeBmpHeader 0x1757c0 98.01 -> 100.00 EXACT
- [`test/je` past the load with NO `xor` on the null arm: that is MFC's `GetSafeHwnd()`](getsafehwnd-null-arm-first.md) - c9 - cpp:ternary cpp:branch mfc:cwnd cpp:inline | asm:test asm:je asm:xor | topic:codegen-idiom - `p ? p->m_hWnd : 0` puts the non-null arm first so cl has to materialise the zero (`jmp J / xor eax,eax`); MFC's inline is `this == NULL ? NULL : m_hWnd`, null arm FIRST, and falls through with eax already zero from the `test`. Spell the NULL arm first for any hand-rolled null-guarded member fetch. CMoviePlayer::CreateVideoWindow 0x17c2a0 98.01 -> 100.00 EXACT (its whole 2-instruction deficit)
- [`case static_cast<i32>(HRESULT)` is CORRECT - do not hoist the cast to the switch](switch-cast-survey-hresult.md) - c10 - cpp:cast cpp:switch | topic:tooling - the 74 `case static_cast<i32>(DIERR_*/DDERR_*/0x8xxxxxxx)` labels in DinMgr2/DirectDrawMgr/SoundStream look like 74 removable casts. They are not. The constants are a MIX of >INT_MAX literals and MAKE_HRESULT macro expansions that are SIGNED NEGATIVE, and the switch variable is genuinely `i32` (retail mangling `?GetErrorString@DirectSoundMgr@@SAXPADHH@Z` = PAD,H,H - widening the param would break the RVA binding). Hoisting to `switch (static_cast<u32>(hr))` compiles under cl 5.0 and is byte-neutral (3 units mean 89.1885 either way) but CLANG REJECTS IT - `case value evaluates to -2005401450, which cannot be narrowed to type 'u32' [-Wc++11-narrowing]` - so gen_labels produces no IR and every function in those three TUs silently vanishes from the label CSV. Tried and reverted 2026-08-07 (58b23518a, reverted). The per-label cast is the only spelling both compilers accept.
- [Two adjacent 8-byte fields serialise through ONE hoisted base, not two `lea`s](pair-transfer-hoists-its-base-above-the-mode-switch.md) - c10 - cpp:switch cpp:member cpp:inline cpp:serialize | asm:lea asm:add asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom - a Serialize/SerializeMove one instruction short: retail has `lea reg,[this+off]` ABOVE the `cmp mode,4` and reaches the second field with `add reg,8`, ours recomputes a `lea` per field inside each arm. A timing pair (two adjacent i64/Clock64) goes through the tree's inline `SerBandPair` (<Gruntz/SerialRecords.h>), not a hand-written two-arm switch. CObjectDropper/CTimeBomb/CActionArea/CAniPlayer/CToyPeek SerializeMove all -> 100 EXACT; CTimer::HandleEvent (TWO pairs) 84.58 -> 99.43. NOT universal: `CPairRecord::Serialize` 0x58ee0 is the out-of-line spelling and is exact AS the switch - decide per site by whether retail's `lea` sits above the `cmp`
- [A store written in BOTH arms and hoisted by cl costs the free `return 0`](store-hoisted-out-of-both-arms-costs-the-zero-eax-return.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:return cpp:member | asm:xor asm:test asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom - exactly one instruction short: retail has `xor eax,eax` before an epilogue where you have none, and an EARLIER guard in the same function omits it. After `test eax,eax/jne`, eax is known 0 on the fall-through only while it is still LIVE at the branch - a store OF eax between them keeps it, a store of another register ends its range and the `return 0` must rematerialize. What puts the unrelated store there is a statement written in both arms that cl hoisted. CCreditsState::InitAttractTitle 0x39570 99.01 -> 100 EXACT
- [A struct cleared with `memset` gets its OWN `xor reg,reg`; field stores borrow an accumulator's zero](memset-clears-with-a-fresh-zero-register.md) - c8 - cpp:local cpp:struct cpp:memset | asm:xor asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - retail materializes a zero for one clear of a small stack struct while reusing a live zero register for another clear of the SAME struct; that mix means two different constructs - a `memset` and a field assignment. cl5 does not coalesce a memset's zero with an accumulator's. HeapStats 0x118bf0 97.34 -> 100 EXACT (its sibling HeapCheckDump already used memset and was exact)
- [`push A / jmp J / push B / J: <shared call>` is TWO calls cross-jumped, not a selected argument](two-calls-cross-jumped-down-to-the-differing-push.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:call cpp:ternary | asm:push asm:jmp asm:cmp | topic:codegen-idiom - retail branches around a single `push <imm>` and falls into a shared argument tail + `call`; no register ever holds the choice. Write the call TWICE in an if/else and let cl merge the tail - a value select gives assign-then-override (`mov eax,B/cmp/jne/mov eax,A`) or, when the constants are close, branchless `neg/sbb/and/add`. The taken branch names the second-written arm. Distinct from default-then-override-flag (there the value outlives the branch). CCreditsState::Render 0x391d0 96.65 -> 100 EXACT
- [enum-switch-selector-lowers-signed](enum-switch-selector-lowers-signed.md) - an MSVC5 `enum` is `int`, so a typed switch selector lowers a SIGNED ladder (`jg`) where retail has `ja`.
- [A `default: return;` we invented is an extra early return - it pins the whole prologue](fabricated-default-return-pins-the-prologue.md) - c10 - cpp:switch cpp:return | asm:push asm:pop | topic:codegen-idiom - a `switch` whose arms only append to a buffer, then one call after it: retail's unmatched value FALLS THROUGH to that call, it does not return. A fabricated `default: return;` is an extra early return, so by shrink-wrapped-prologue-needs-one-tail-return it pins every callee-saved push into the prologue and rotates the whole allocation - the cost looks nothing like a switch bug. Point `--blocks --lite` at the switch's last `jne` and see where the unmatched value lands. LoadVideoResolutionConfig 0x36f30 97.27 -> 100 EXACT, SaveVideoResolutionConfig 0x370a0 88.81 -> 100 EXACT (retail's prologue there is a bare `sub esp,0x40`, all four saves inside the body)
- [A guarded first half that `return`s leaves the rest at FUNCTION scope - and its locals stop sharing slots](second-half-at-function-scope-cannot-share-a-frame.md) - c9 - cpp:branch cpp:local | asm:sub asm:lea | topic:codegen-idiom - every `[esp+N]` shifted by a constant and `sub esp,X` off by exactly one local's size, block skeleton otherwise identical. cl 5.0 overlaps stack slots by BLOCK SCOPE, not live range: `if (c) { locals A; return 1; } locals B; ... return 1;` puts B at function scope where it cannot alias A, costing a whole extra slot group. Retail's packing means the source had them as siblings - `if (c) { ... return 1; } else { ... return 1; }`. A frame-size mismatch is never noise; count the delta in units of sizeof(the suspect local). CBootyState::ShowSecretBonusMessage 0x18f00 frame 0x58 -> 0x48, 93.19 -> 100 EXACT
- [We declared `i32` where retail declared `void` - and it costs the whole prologue](int-return-that-retail-never-sets-is-a-void.md) - c9 - cpp:return | asm:xor asm:ret | topic:codegen-idiom - the INVERSE of `gruntz.audit.void_return_type`: base has `xor eax,eax`/`mov eax,1` before a ret that retail lacks, retail's ret blocks leave eax holding the last callee's result, and the spurious return value costs a register and blocks the shrink-wrap. Confirm with `--target --lite | grep eax` (no constant reaches eax at any ret) AND `sema xref` (every caller discards it) - a caller that USES the result refutes it, e.g. CGameLevel::MoveRising looks identical but is a genuine int whose value is already in eax. Flipping rewrites the mangled name, so update the header decl and all call sites in one change. CTriggerMgr::ReinitGroup 0x79b80 83.06 -> 97.31, then 100 EXACT once the else-arm is `m_byteArr.Add(x)` and NOT `SetAtGrow(GetSize(), x)` (measured both ways). **Now a sieve: `python -m gruntz.audit.int_return_type`** - the conjunction of both conditions, because condition 1 alone is a 90% false-positive rate; calibrated at 0 false fires over 786 declared-void 100%-exact fns. Two traps it had to learn: read `%al` too (a `bool` return is materialised byte-wide, else the whole CButeMgr `_N` family reads as void) and bound the backward walk by the BASIC BLOCK not an instruction count (CBootyState::LoadGameAssetNamespaces parks `mov eax,1` exactly 10 insns before its ret)
- [The "dead 8-byte `Coord` temp" is a REGISTER-ALLOCATOR SPILL, not a source local](dead-eight-byte-coord-temp-is-unreproduced.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:struct | asm:sub asm:mov | topic:wall topic:scoring-artifact - the entire frame is `sub esp,0x8`, written by two `mov [esp+k],reg` and never read; our base has no frame at all, so one callee-saved push and every `[esp+N]` differ. Seen in CTriggerMgr::NotifyCell 0x79fb0, CTriggerMgr::ToggleRegionA 0x7d450, CGrunt::ResolveArrivalNeighbor 0xf26f0. **The file used to say the source "clearly had" a `Coord` local; that was WRONG and cost three lanes.** In each one the two "copied" values are ALSO kept in registers and every use reads them from there (`mov [esp+0x10],edx` beside `mov ecx,edx`) - a spill pair the allocator emitted and never needed. Corroboration: 39 of the 75 retail fns whose whole frame is `sub esp,0x8` are already 100% for us and none declares an 8-byte local. 38 probe cells (the 9 documented + 29 more: live copy-init, by-value return under POD/ctor/copy-ctor, inline out-param accessor, by-value struct arg, union, array, aggregate init, ...) all deleted it; only `volatile`, a real out-of-line `&p` escape, or a user dtor keep it, and the last two emit code retail lacks. Treat as a regalloc wall - permuter, not another construct hunt
- [MSVC 5.0 has no `operator new[]` - the allocation SYMBOL cannot tell you the source form](msvc5-has-no-array-new-read-the-vector-ctor.md) - c10 - cpp:new cpp:array cpp:class | asm:push asm:call | topic:codegen-idiom topic:identity - `??_U@YAPAXI@Z` appears ZERO times in MSVC 5.0's LIBCMT, so `new T[n]`, `new BYTE[n*sizeof(T)]` and `::operator new(n*sizeof(T))` all lower to the same `push <n>; call ??2@YAPAXI@Z`. What reads out is the VECTOR CTOR: `new T[n]` for a T with a user ctor adds a /GX EH frame, a `.text$x` funclet and a `??0T` / `??_H` loop (no cookie - MSVC5 only needs one when T has a DTOR), so retail allocating exactly n*sizeof(T) with no `??0T` after the call PROVES a raw byte allocation (MFC CArray's `(TYPE*)new BYTE[n*sizeof(TYPE)]`; elements built later by a placement-new `ConstructElements` loop). The steerable half is the DELETE: `::operator delete(p)` and `delete[] p` are byte-identical in isolation but colour the following block differently - retail's `SetSize` tail wants the statement form. CRezBufferObject::SetSize 95.90 -> 96.04, Serialize 96.47 -> 97.39, CFaderMesh::ApplyInit 68.30 -> 69.79, nothing else in the tree moved. Corollary: a per-class `operator new` that hardcodes a size inverts the class-size oracle and hides layout bugs from class_sizes - delete it, cl inlines a forwarder away and emits no `??2CFoo@@SAPAXI@Z`
- [A `Mgr* m = m_member;` local retail never had: one extra callee-save push, every register rotated](invented-member-pointer-local.md) - c9 - cpp:local cpp:member | asm:push asm:mov | topic:codegen-idiom topic:regalloc - base pushes one MORE callee-saved register than the target and holds `[this+N]` in it for the whole body; retail re-issues `mov reg,[this+N]` at every use, so the recompile is 2-3 instructions SHORTER per block and one push LONGER at entry with everything after it rotated. Reads like regalloc noise, is not. Screen with `python -m gruntz.audit.shrink_wrap` (a `+1` row) then grep the named function for `T* x = m_member;`. Delete the local, spell `m_member->` at every use: CMulti::PumpB 0xb6e90 83.38 -> 92.36 on that edit alone. Corollary from the same function's residue: `&rc` escaping into `SetRect` means cl can neither DSE a duplicated `rc.top = g->cy;` nor CSE the global field reads across it - so a repeated store to one escaped slot is REAL SOURCE, transcribe it (92.36 -> 98.32); placing that second store between the two field reads instead measures worse (97.66)
